Esse projeto ja deu certo, e ja foi validado, precisamos, desenvolver com o que temos. achei esse documento a seguir.: # Running Claude Code Free On Your Laptop

Hey, here's the setup that's been going around for running Claude Code completely free on your laptop.

Quick honest note up front: I'm not personally using this yet. Frontier models are still ahead for the kind of work I'm doing right now. But the gap is closing fast, and if you want to save some money on the subscription, this is one of the cleanest setups I've come across. Passing it along.

The setup takes about 10 minutes if you've never opened a terminal. Less if you have.

## Setup, step by step

You'll use your terminal once. On Mac, press `Command + Space`, type "Terminal", hit enter. That's it, you're in.

### Step 1, install Ollama

Go to [ollama.com](http://ollama.com/), click download, install like any other app. It runs quietly in the background. You won't notice it's there.

### Step 2, pull a coding model

In your terminal, paste one of these and hit enter:

If your laptop is newer (last 3-4 years, with decent RAM):

```
ollama pull qwen3-coder:30b
```

If your laptop is older or lower-spec:

```
ollama pull qwen2.5-coder:1.5b
```

Takes a few minutes to download. You only do this once. The 30B model is more capable, the 1.5B runs on almost anyt
<truncated 1944 bytes>
 They're not, at least not yet.

What the local model handles fine:

- Bug fixes and small functions
- Refactoring and cleaning up code
- "Explain this code" questions
- Routine tasks where accuracy is good-enough not perfect

Where you'll feel the gap:

- Complex multi-file reasoning
- Large codebases with cross-file dependencies
- Architectural problems that need frontier-level thinking

The gap is real. Local handles routine tasks well. Don't expect it to replace Sonnet on hard architectural problems. The trade-off is no cost and complete privacy in exchange for a less capable model.

## Who this setup is for

**Use it if:**

- You travel and need to code offline (planes, remote spots, no signal)
- You're hitting API overages on Pro and want a free fallback for routine work
- You want to keep your code completely off the cloud
- You want to understand local AI inference before it becomes a real business decision

**Skip it if:**

- Your work needs frontier-quality multi-file reasoning where accuracy matters more than cost
- You're not comfortable opening a terminal at all
- Your laptop is more than 5-6 years old without a dedicated GPU

**Understanding the Goals**

I'm focusing on the user's requirements: configuring local Ollama for OpenClaw's GitHub integration (branching, committing, pushing), and integrating paid model tiers/pricing. I'm investigating how OpenClaw interacts with these systems.


**Investigating Model Configuration**

I've been examining `openclaw.json` for model configuration. It looks like we can customize the primary model, defaulting to Google's Gemini, by modifying the `model.primary` field. I see we could switch to a local Ollama model like `ollama/qwen3.5:latest` or `ollama/llama3.1:8b`. This would be defined in the `models.providers.ollama` section.

> 1: -- Migration: 20260527012945_agent_metering_minimal
> 2: -- CEA-A · Constellation Economic Activation — schema mínimo para metering de tokens/custo por agent.
> 3: -- Princípio: começar pequeno. Apenas o necessário para registrar 1 execução real e calcular cost_mcoin.
> 4: -- RLS default deny + user_id isolation. Pricing table seed com providers comuns.
> 5: 
> 6: -- ===== agent_executions =====
> 7: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.agent_executions (
> 8:   id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 9:   agent_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.crew_agents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 10:   user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 11:   prompt text NOT NULL,
> 12:   response text,
> 13:   tokens_in int DEFAULT 0,
> 14:   tokens_out int DEFAULT 0,
> 15:   cost_usd_micro bigint DEFAULT 0,
> 16:   cost_mcoin numeric(12,4) DEFAULT 0,
> 17:   provider text NOT NULL,
> 18:   model text NOT NULL,
> 19:   status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending','running','done','failed')),
> 20:   error_msg text,
> 21:   latency_ms int,
> 22:   started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
> 23:   completed_at timestamptz
> 24: );
> 25: 
> 26: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_user_started ON public.agent_executions (user_id, started_at DESC);
> 27: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_agent_started ON public.agent_executions (agent_id, started_at DESC);
> 28: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_status ON public.agent_executions (status) WHERE status IN ('pending', 'running');
> 29: 
> 30: ALTER TABLE pu
> <truncated 3176 bytes>
> margin numeric DEFAULT 2.0
> 88: ) RETURNS jsonb
> 89: LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 90: STABLE
> 91: AS $$
> 92: DECLARE
> 93:   v_price_in numeric;
> 94:   v_price_out numeric;
> 95:   v_cost_cents numeric;
> 96:   v_cost_usd_micro bigint;
> 97:   v_cost_mcoin numeric;
> 98: BEGIN
> 99:   SELECT price_in_cents_per_mtok, price_out_cents_per_mtok
> 100:   INTO v_price_in, v_price_out
> 101:   FROM public.agent_pricing
> 102:   WHERE provider = p_provider AND model = p_model;
> 103: 
> 104:   IF v_price_in IS NULL THEN
> 105:     RETURN jsonb_build_object(
> 106:       'error', 'pricing_not_found',
> 107:       'provider', p_provider,
> 108:       'model', p_model
> 109:     );
> 110:   END IF;
> 111: 
> 112:   v_cost_cents := (p_tokens_in * v_price_in + p_tokens_out * v_price_out) / 1000000.0;
> 113:   v_cost_usd_micro := (v_cost_cents * 10000)::bigint;  -- cents * 10000 = micro-USD
> 114:   v_cost_mcoin := GREATEST(CEIL(v_cost_cents / 100.0 * p_margin * 1000), 1);  -- min 1 mcoCoin
> 115: 
> 116:   RETURN jsonb_build_object(
> 117:     'tokens_in', p_tokens_in,
> 118:     'tokens_out', p_tokens_out,
> 119:     'price_in_cents_per_mtok', v_price_in,
> 120:     'price_out_cents_per_mtok', v_price_out,
> 121:     'cost_cents', v_cost_cents,
> 122:     'cost_usd_micro', v_cost_usd_micro,
> 123:     'cost_mcoin', v_cost_mcoin,
> 124:     'margin', p_margin
> 125:   );
> 126: END;
> 127: $$;
> 128: 
> 129: GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.calc_agent_cost_mcoin(int, int, text, text, numeric) TO authenticated, service_role;
> 130: 
> 131: COMMENT ON TABLE public.agent_executions IS 'CEA-A · Token + cost ledger por execução de agente da Constelação. Source for /dashboard/agent-economics.';
> 132: COMMENT ON TABLE public.agent_pricing IS 'CEA-A · Tabela de preços por (provider, model). Cents per 1M tokens. Updated as provider rates change.';
> 133: COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.calc_agent_cost_mcoin IS 'CEA-A · Cost calculator. Formula: tokens * price_per_mtok = cents; cents/100 * MARGIN * 1000 = mcoCoin. Min 1 mcoCoin per exec.';

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

**Extending Agent Functionality**

I'm realizing the `agent-task-execute` Edge Function is currently limited, only supporting `groq`, `openrouter`, and `openai`. A crucial next step is figuring out how to extend this to support `ollama`. This seems like the primary challenge to solve. I believe it would significantly enhance the agent's capabilities.


**Investigating Localhost Access**

I'm now diving deeper into how `localhost` resolves in different contexts. It's becoming clearer that `localhost` within the Supabase Edge Function environment points to the Deno container, not the host machine. This means connecting to a locally running Ollama server at `http://localhost:11434` won't work directly without some configuration. However, OpenClaw, running natively, *can* access it via `localhost`. The same is true for the Antigravity IDE and Gemini/Claude.

</details>

> 1: # UX/UI Senior Refactoring and Style Settings Inheritance for Canvas Studio
> 2: 
> 3: This plan details the senior-level UX/UI refactoring of the Canvas Studio Right Panel Inspectors and the integration of project-level Style Settings (mood, palette, preset) inheritance for canvas node executions.
> 4: 
> 5: Currently, project-level styles defined in the `StyleSettingsPanel` (when no node is selected) are completely ignored during execution because they are not passed to the `resolveExecutePayload` function. Furthermore, the UI controls in the sidebar lack consistent premium aesthetics, visual feedback, and clear cues for project-level inheritance.

We will add a toggle in the UI so users can choose to override/bypass project-level styles for specific nodes.

> 1: -- Migration: 20260527012945_agent_metering_minimal
> 2: -- CEA-A · Constellation Economic Activation — schema mínimo para metering de tokens/custo por agent.
> 3: -- Princípio: começar pequeno. Apenas o necessário para registrar 1 execução real e calcular cost_mcoin.
> 4: -- RLS default deny + user_id isolation. Pricing table seed com providers comuns.
> 5: 
> 6: -- ===== agent_executions =====
> 7: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.agent_executions (
> 8:   id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
> 9:   agent_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.crew_agents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 10:   user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
> 11:   prompt text NOT NULL,
> 12:   response text,
> 13:   tokens_in int DEFAULT 0,
> 14:   tokens_out int DEFAULT 0,
> 15:   cost_usd_micro bigint DEFAULT 0,
> 16:   cost_mcoin numeric(12,4) DEFAULT 0,
> 17:   provider text NOT NULL,
> 18:   model text NOT NULL,
> 19:   status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending','running','done','failed')),
> 20:   error_msg text,
> 21:   latency_ms int,
> 22:   started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
> 23:   completed_at timestamptz
> 24: );
> 25: 
> 26: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_user_started ON public.agent_executions (user_id, started_at DESC);
> 27: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_agent_started ON public.agent_executions (agent_id, started_at DESC);
> 28: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_executions_status ON public.agent_executions (status) WHERE status IN ('pending', 'running');
> 29: 
> 30: ALTER TABLE pu
> <truncated 3176 bytes>
> margin numeric DEFAULT 2.0
> 88: ) RETURNS jsonb
> 89: LANGUAGE plpgsql
> 90: STABLE
> 91: AS $$
> 92: DECLARE
> 93:   v_price_in numeric;
> 94:   v_price_out numeric;
> 95:   v_cost_cents numeric;
> 96:   v_cost_usd_micro bigint;
> 97:   v_cost_mcoin numeric;
> 98: BEGIN
> 99:   SELECT price_in_cents_per_mtok, price_out_cents_per_mtok
> 100:   INTO v_price_in, v_price_out
> 101:   FROM public.agent_pricing
> 102:   WHERE provider = p_provider AND model = p_model;
> 103: 
> 104:   IF v_price_in IS NULL THEN
> 105:     RETURN jsonb_build_object(
> 106:       'error', 'pricing_not_found',
> 107:       'provider', p_provider,
> 108:       'model', p_model
> 109:     );
> 110:   END IF;
> 111: 
> 112:   v_cost_cents := (p_tokens_in * v_price_in + p_tokens_out * v_price_out) / 1000000.0;
> 113:   v_cost_usd_micro := (v_cost_cents * 10000)::bigint;  -- cents * 10000 = micro-USD
> 114:   v_cost_mcoin := GREATEST(CEIL(v_cost_cents / 100.0 * p_margin * 1000), 1);  -- min 1 mcoCoin
> 115: 
> 116:   RETURN jsonb_build_object(
> 117:     'tokens_in', p_tokens_in,
> 118:     'tokens_out', p_tokens_out,
> 119:     'price_in_cents_per_mtok', v_price_in,
> 120:     'price_out_cents_per_mtok', v_price_out,
> 121:     'cost_cents', v_cost_cents,
> 122:     'cost_usd_micro', v_cost_usd_micro,
> 123:     'cost_mcoin', v_cost_mcoin,
> 124:     'margin', p_margin
> 125:   );
> 126: END;
> 127: $$;
> 128: 
> 129: GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.calc_agent_cost_mcoin(int, int, text, text, numeric) TO authenticated, service_role;
> 130: 
> 131: COMMENT ON TABLE public.agent_executions IS 'CEA-A · Token + cost ledger por execução de agente da Constelação. Source for /dashboard/agent-economics.';
> 132: COMMENT ON TABLE public.agent_pricing IS 'CEA-A · Tabela de preços por (provider, model). Cents per 1M tokens. Updated as provider rates change.';
> 133: COMMENT ON FUNCTION public.calc_agent_cost_mcoin IS 'CEA-A · Cost calculator. Formula: tokens * price_per_mtok = cents; cents/100 * MARGIN * 1000 = mcoCoin. Min 1 mcoCoin per exec.';

**Determining Ollama Endpoint**

I've realized that within the Docker container, `localhost` refers to the container, not the host. This means I must use `http://host.docker.internal:11434` or an environment variable. OpenClaw's default `baseUrl` won't work in Docker without host network mode or a Docker bridge route. I've decided to allow both `OLLAMA_HOST` and a fallback strategy for determining the correct endpoint. Now I'm focused on verifying the expected Ollama API endpoint, which is `/api/chat`.
